front.1.blocks.47.text.ja	ワークブックⅡの構成と内容
front.1.blocks.48.text.ja	ワークブックには、テキスト各課の「読み物」と「文型・表現ノート」に関する練習問題があります。
front.1.blocks.49.items.0.ja	**読み物ワーク**　「読み物1」「読み物2」それぞれについて、A. 読み物の内容に関する正誤問題、B. 読みのストラテジーの問題、C. 内容についてより詳しく問う質問タイプの問題、の3つを用意しました。
front.1.blocks.49.items.1.ja	**文型・表現ワーク**　「文型・表現ノート」の項目に関する練習問題には2タイプあり、Aはアウトプットまで求める項目（テキストで★がついているもの）に関する基本問題、Bはその課で出ているすべての文型・表現を網羅したまとめの問題です。
front.1.blocks.50.text.ja	また、テキストの「ブラッシュアップ」にある「上級へのチャレンジ」と「漢字チャレンジ」の練習問題も用意しました。「**上級へのチャレンジ ワーク**」と「**漢字チャレンジ ワーク**」は、テキストで学んだ後にその確認として行ってください。
front.2.title.en	About This Book
front.2.blocks.0.text.en	What’s Quartet?
front.2.blocks.1.text.en	Quartet: Intermediate Japanese Across the Four Language Skills is a comprehensive intermediate learning resource for Japanese-language learners who have completed around 250 to 300 hours of elementary study. It is designed to provide well-balanced development of the four language skills—reading, writing, speaking and listening—through the study of grammar, expressions and strategies needed to communicate at the intermediate level.
front.2.blocks.2.text.en	Quartet is divided into two textbook volumes, each with a supplement, a workbook, and downloadable audio material. Volume I presents Lessons 1–6, and Volume II covers Lessons 7–12. Together, the two volumes are intended to help learners advance to level B1 of the Common European Framework of Reference for Languages (CEFR).
front.2.blocks.3.text.en	The material in Volume I is roughly equivalent to level N3 of the Japanese Language Proficiency Test, while that in Volume II is on par with N2 and N1. Each lesson focuses on approximately ten grammatical patterns and expressions, around 45 kanji, and one or two reading strategies. In total, Volume II presents 65 grammatical patterns and expressions, 330 kanji, and ten reading strategies.
front.2.blocks.4.labels.0	Volume I (Textbook)
front.2.blocks.4.labels.1	(Supplement)
front.2.blocks.4.labels.2	Workbook I
front.2.blocks.4.labels.3	Audio
front.2.blocks.4.labels.4	(Download)
front.2.blocks.4.labels.5	Volume II (Textbook)
front.2.blocks.4.labels.6	(Supplement)
front.2.blocks.4.labels.7	Workbook II
front.2.blocks.4.labels.8	Audio
front.2.blocks.4.labels.9	(Download)
front.2.blocks.5.text.en	Structure and Content of Volume II
front.2.blocks.6.text.en	Every textbook lesson has four sections for the four language skills—**読む**, **書く**, **話す** and **聞く**—with each sharing the same theme. The sections are interconnected in various ways. For instance, the topic of the readings in 読む is taken up in the compositions of 書く, the dialogues of 話す, and the listening comprehension material of 聞く. As another example, the grammatical patterns and expressions used in the readings are also encountered in the listening comprehension tasks. This interlinking enables you to build up the four skills in a natural, well-balanced manner. The vocabulary and kanji lists have been compiled into a separate supplement (**{別冊|べっさつ}**) so that they can be viewed alongside the textbook material. The textbook’s six lessons are followed by a section entitled ブラッシュアップ, which offers activities for expanding your range of expression and a section on kanji.
front.2.blocks.7.text.en	The structure and content of Volume II are as follows.
front.2.blocks.8.text.en	(1) Lessons 7–12
front.2.blocks.9.text.en	Study grammatical patterns expressions and reading strategies through two readings
front.2.blocks.10.items.0.en	**読む前に・読んだ後で**　**読む前に** prepares you for each reading with schema activation in the form of questions on the reading’s topic. **読んだ後で** involves tasks such as summarizing the reading in your own words, comparing the content with the situation in your homeland, and discussing your experiences and opinions in relation to the reading.
front.2.blocks.10.items.1.en	**読み物**　Each lesson has two readings (**読み物 1**／**読み物 2**). The readings gradually become more challenging with each new lesson, and generally focus on topics concerning Japanese culture and society that are of interest to many students. All the readings in Volume II other than the first one in Lesson 8 are real-world passages that haven’t been watered down for learners. This is intended to give learners a greater sense of accomplishment after they complete each passage, and thus be more strongly motivated to advance their reading skills.
front.2.blocks.10.items.2.en	**読みのストラテジー**　This part presents reading strategies that not only are useful for developing a better understanding of the readings, but can also be effectively applied to reading comprehension in general. As with Volume I, the strategies covered in Volume II offer tips on how to parse sentences and follow the structure of compositions.
front.2.blocks.10.items.3.en	**{文型|ぶんけい}・表現ノート**　Commentary is provided on the grammatical patterns and expressions used in the readings. Volume II covers mainly intermediate patterns and expressions, centering on JLPT N2 and N1 grammar. This section is designed to foster your ability to understand difficult words and sentences from context, offering explanations in English and numerous example sentences to assist you. Items marked with ★ are ones that you should strive to master for spoken/written communication, while those not so marked are ones that you just need to understand the meaning.
front.2.blocks.11.text.en	Write compositions based on the model text
front.2.blocks.12.text.en	This section challenges you to use what you learned in 読む to write short compositions on subjects related to the readings. Before that, you go over **モデル作文** and **書くポイント** to acquire a better understanding of text structure and writing pointers, and you respond to the questions in **書いてみよう** to brainstorm for your composition.
front.2.blocks.13.text.en	Practice conversation patterns using a model dialogue
front.2.blocks.14.text.en	This section is divided into two parts, **会話 1** and **会話 2**. Each part offers conversation practice that is based on a model dialogue and progresses from input to output. 会話 1 presents task-based dialogues, such as inquiring about a lost article or making a suggestion. 会話 2 focuses on monologues such as speeches or presentations and discussions.
front.2.blocks.15.blocks.0.items.0.text.en	**やってみよう**　Before going through the model dialogue, you do a little role-playing in the dialogue’s context to see how well you can communicate in that situation.
front.2.blocks.15.blocks.0.items.1.text.en	**聞いてみよう**　As you listen to the model dialogue, you try to understand its content and flow.
front.2.blocks.15.blocks.0.items.2.text.en	**モデル会話**　Next, you read a script of the dialogue to check your understanding. フローチャート serves as a visual aid for gaining a handle on the overall flow of the dialogue.
front.2.blocks.15.blocks.0.items.3.text.en	**練習しよう**　You then practice creating output in your own words, following patterns of expression drawn from the dialogue.
front.2.blocks.16.text.en	Practice two types of listening comprehension
front.2.blocks.17.text.en	聞く is also divided into two parts, **{聴解|ちょうかい} 1** and **聴解 2**, both of which offer content related to the readings. 聴解 1 provides listening comprehension practice that requires you to identify specific information from a lecture, explanation, or other material that you listen to while looking as figures or charts. 聴解 2 presents conversations in which an international student discusses social topics with a friend. The subject matter lends well to discussion, so this material can also be used for discussion after listening comprehension practice.
front.2.blocks.18.text.en	(2) Brush-ups (ブラッシュアップ)
front.2.blocks.19.text.en	Lessons 7–12 are followed by a separate section that provides activities for expanding your range of expression, and handy strategies for mastering kanji.
front.2.blocks.20.text.en	上級へのチャレンジ
front.2.blocks.21.text.en	This part presents composition-enriching expressions from the readings to help you further build your vocabulary and expression skills. Some of these items can be reviewed more effectively when studied along with certain parts of the lessons. The table below lists those pairings. The last one, ⑧インタビュープロジェクト, is a project that challenges you to utilize what you learned in Lessons 8, 10 and 12.
front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.head.0.0.en	上級へのチャレンジ
front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.head.0.1.en	Corresponding sections of lessons
front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.rows.0.0.en	① 視点

front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.rows.6.1.en	☛ 第12課：読み物2
front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.rows.7.0.en	⑧ インタビュープロジェクト
front.2.blocks.22.blocks.0.rows.7.1.en	☛ 第8課：書く／第10課：書く／第12課：書く
front.2.blocks.23.text.en	漢字チャレンジ
front.2.blocks.24.text.en	This section presents 12 strategies essential for kanji studies at the intermediate level and beyond, including strategies concerning radicals and phonetic indicators providing clues to the kanji readings. By equipping yourself with these strategies, you’ll have an easier time surmising the meaning and reading of unfamiliar kanji and tidy up your knowledge of kanji already learned. This, in turn, will enable you to efficiently expand the kanji knowledge and skills that will become all the more important from the intermediate level onward.
front.2.blocks.25.text.en	(3) Appendices (巻末)
front.2.blocks.26.items.0.en	**{聴解|ちょうかい} 解答・スクリプト**　This appendix contains the answers to the two listening comprehension tasks in each 聞く section and full scripts of the audio material. It provides the correct answers to multiple-choice and true/false questions, and model answers for the writing problems.
front.2.blocks.26.items.1.en	**{文型|ぶんけい}・表現さくいん**　This is a list of the items presented in 文型・表現ノート, arranged in a-i-u-e-o order.
front.2.blocks.26.items.2.en	**単語さくいん**　This lists the words included on the supplement’s vocabulary list, in a-i-u-e-o order.
front.2.blocks.27.text.en	(4) Supplement ({別冊|べっさつ})
front.2.blocks.28.text.en	The supplement accompanying the textbook contains vocabulary lists, lists of words that should be memorized and sample sentences using them, and kanji lists.
front.2.blocks.29.items.0.en	**単語リスト**　These list the vocabulary in the readings that have not yet been studied. The words in each list that should especially be learned are presented again in the subsequent **覚える単語と例文** list, along with sample sentences using them.
front.2.blocks.29.items.1.en	**漢字リスト**　These lists contain the target kanji found in the reading material, along with their readings, meanings, stroke orders, compounds and English translations. Shaded characters are essential kanji that you need to learn how to write.

front.2.blocks.41.text.en	Structure and Content of Workbook II
front.2.blocks.42.text.en	The workbook offers practice exercises related to each textbook lesson’s 読み物 and {文型|ぶんけい}・{表現|ひょうげん}ノート.
front.2.blocks.43.items.0.en	**読み物ワーク**　This section provides three types of exercises for 読み物 1 and 2——(A) true/false questions on the readings, (B) reading strategy exercises, and (C) questions that delve into the readings’ content in greater detail.
front.2.blocks.43.items.1.en	**{文型|ぶんけい}・表現ワーク**　Two sets of exercises are provided for the items covered by 文型・表現ノート——(A) basic problems on items that require output (marked with ★ in the textbook), and (B) exercises covering all grammatical patterns and expressions presented in the lesson.
front.2.blocks.44.text.en	The workbook also includes practices for the 上級へのチャレンジ and 漢字チャレンジ sections of ブラッシュアップ in the textbook. The worksheets should be used after studying the corresponding part of the textbook in order to check your mastery of the kanji.
